============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- .next.*
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
     2	import { EB_Garamond } from "next/font/google";
     3	import { ScreenshotPreviewLayer } from "./ScreenshotPreviewLayer";
     4	import "./globals.css";
     5	
     6	const ebGaramond = EB_Garamond({
     7	  subsets: ["latin"],
     8	  style: "italic",
     9	  variable: "--font-eb-garamond",
    10	  weight: "500",
    11	});
    12	
    13	export const metadata: Metadata = {
    14	  metadataBase: new URL(
    15	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || "https://prahl.dev",
    16	  ),
    17	  title: "Prahl.dev | React Native Full Stack Mobile Developer",
    18	  description:
    19	    "Portfolio for Prahl.dev, a React Native full stack mobile developer building polished mobile apps, secure checkout flows, and production-ready Android pipelines.",
    20	  openGraph: {
    21	    title: "Prahl.dev | React Native Full Stack Mobile Developer",
    22	    description:
    23	      "React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, and Kotlin mobile portfolio.",
    24	    images: ["/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png"],
    25	  },
    26	};
    27	
    28	export const viewport: Viewport = {
    29	  width: "device-width",
    30	  initialScale: 1,
    31	};
    32	
    33	export default function RootLayout({
    34	  children,
    35	}: Readonly<{
    36	  children: React.ReactNode;
    37	}>) {
    38	  return (
    39	    <html lang="en">
    40	      <body className={ebGaramond.variable}>
    41	        {children}
    42	        <ScreenshotPreviewLayer />
    43	      </body>
    44	    </html>
    45	  );
    46	}

    61	
    62	function StackTerm({
    63	  icon,
    64	  imageAlt,
    65	  imageSrc,
    66	  iconStroke,
    67	  label,
    68	  postmark = false,
    69	  symbol: Symbol,
    70	  symbolLabel,
    71	}: {
    72	  icon?: SimpleIcon;
    73	  imageAlt?: string;
    74	  imageSrc?: string;
    75	  iconStroke?: string;
    76	  label: string;
    77	  postmark?: boolean;
    78	  symbol?: LucideIcon;
    79	  symbolLabel?: string;
    80	}) {
    81	  const iconColor =
    82	    icon?.title === "Gradle"
    83	      ? "#9fd7dc"
    84	      : icon?.title === "PayPal"
    85	        ? "#35bdf2"
    86	      : icon?.hex === "000000" || icon?.hex === "1C2024"
    87	      ? "#f3fff7"
    88	      : icon
    89	        ? `#${icon.hex}`
    90	        : undefined;
    91	
    92	  return (
    93	    <li>
    94	      {imageSrc ? (
    95	        <Image
    96	          alt={imageAlt ?? ""}
    97	          className="capability-brand-mark"
    98	          height={24}
    99	          src={imageSrc}
   100	          width={24}
   101	        />
   102	      ) : postmark ? (
   103	        <svg
   104	          aria-label="Postmark logo"
   105	          className="capability-brand-mark"
   106	          role="img"
   107	          viewBox="0 0 30 30"
   108	        >
   109	          <path
   110	            clipRule="evenodd"
   111	            d="M30 27.422v1.406A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 28.828 30h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0H8.672a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0H4.922a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0H1.172A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 28.828v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344V8.672a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344V4.922a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344V1.172A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 1.172 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 30 1.172v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344Z"
   112	            fill="#ffde00"
   113	            fillRule="evenodd"
   114	          />
   115	          <path
   116	            d="M8.703 21.38h.935c.481 0 .784-.31.784-.8V9.628c0-.49-.303-.8-.784-.8h-.935V6.4h6.775C19.118 6.4 22 8.286 22 11.876c0 3.616-2.882 5.501-6.522 5.501h-2.326v3.203c0 .49.304.8.81.8h1.895v2.454H8.703V21.38Zm6.573-6.534c2.401 0 3.792-1.033 3.792-2.918 0-1.938-1.39-2.919-3.792-2.919h-2.124v5.863h2.124v-.026Z"
   117	            fill="#000"
   118	          />
   119	        </svg>
   120	      ) : Symbol ? (
   121	        <Symbol
   122	          aria-label={symbolLabel ?? `${label} symbol`}
   123	          className="capability-brand-mark"
   124	          role="img"
   125	        />
   126	      ) : icon ? (
   127	        <svg
   128	          aria-label={`${icon.title} logo`}
   129	          className="capability-brand-mark"
   130	          role="img"
   131	          style={{ color: iconColor }}
   132	          viewBox={iconStroke ? "-1 -1 26 26" : "0 0 24 24"}
   133	        >
   134	          <path
   135	            d={icon.path}
   136	            fill="currentColor"
   137	            paintOrder={iconStroke ? "stroke fill" : undefined}
   138	            stroke={iconStroke}
   139	            strokeLinejoin={iconStroke ? "round" : undefined}
   140	            strokeWidth={iconStroke ? 1.1 : undefined}
   141	          />
   142	        </svg>
   143	      ) : (
   144	        <span className="capability-brand-spacer" aria-hidden="true" />
   145	      )}
   146	      <span>{label}</span>
   147	    </li>
   148	  );
   149	}

   960	function PhonePreview({
   961	  title,
   962	  metric,
   963	  variant,
   964	  href,
   965	  screenImageSrc,
   966	  screenImageAlt,
   967	}: {
   968	  href?: string;
   969	  title: string;
   970	  metric: string;
   971	  variant: "commerce" | "finance";
   972	  screenImageSrc?: string;
   973	  screenImageAlt?: string;
   974	}) {
   975	  const className = `phone-shell phone-shell-${variant}${
   976	    screenImageSrc ? " phone-shell-capture" : ""
   977	  }${href ? " phone-shell-link" : ""}`;
   978	  const content = screenImageSrc ? (
   979	    <Image
   980	      alt={screenImageAlt ?? `${title} app screen`}
   981	      className="phone-framed-image"
   982	      fill
   983	      priority
   984	      sizes="245px"
   985	      src={screenImageSrc}
   986	    />
   987	  ) : (
   988	    <>
   989	      <div className="phone-speaker" />
   990	      <div className="phone-screen">
   991	        <div className="phone-status">
   992	          <span>{title}</span>
   993	          <span>5G</span>
   994	        </div>
   995	        <div className="phone-chart">
   996	          <div />
   997	          <div />
   998	          <div />
   999	          <div />
  1000	        </div>
  1001	        <div className="phone-cardline wide" />
  1002	        <div className="phone-cardline" />
  1003	        <div className="phone-grid">
  1004	          <span />
  1005	          <span />
  1006	          <span />
  1007	          <span />
  1008	        </div>
  1009	        <div className="phone-cta">{metric}</div>
  1010	      </div>
  1011	    </>
  1012	  );
  1013	
  1014	  if (href) {
  1015	    return (
  1016	      <a
  1017	        aria-label={`Open ${title} case study`}
  1018	        className={className}
  1019	        href={href}
  1020	      >
  1021	        {content}
  1022	      </a>
  1023	    );
  1024	  }
  1025	
  1026	  return <div className={className}>{content}</div>;
  1027	}
  1028

  1042	        src="/images/cinerific-hero-tablet-tab-s7-uniform.png"
  1043	      />
  1044	    </a>
  1045	  );
  1046	}

    74	function getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset() {
    75	  const isWideLandscape = window.matchMedia(
    76	    "(min-width: 1024px) and (orientation: landscape)",
    77	  ).matches;
    78	  const isPortrait = window.matchMedia("(orientation: portrait)").matches;
    79	
    80	  if (isWideLandscape) {
    81	    return Math.max(18, Math.min(44, window.innerHeight * 0.035));
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  if (isPortrait) {
    85	    return Math.max(14, Math.min(36, window.innerHeight * 0.03));
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  return Math.max(16, Math.min(40, window.innerHeight * 0.0325));
    89	}
    90	
    91	function scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(caseStudyId: string) {
    92	  const targetCard = document.getElementById(caseStudyId);
    93	
    94	  if (!targetCard) {
    95	    return;
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  const caseGrid = targetCard.closest<HTMLElement>(".case-grid");
    99	  const targetTop = targetCard.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   100	  const rowCards = caseGrid
   101	    ? Array.from(caseGrid.children).filter((child): child is HTMLElement => {
   102	        if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement)) {
   103	          return false;
   104	        }
   105	
   106	        const cardTop = child.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   107	
   108	        return Math.abs(cardTop - targetTop) <= 8;
   109	      })
   110	    : [targetCard];
   111	  const rowTop = Math.min(
   112	    ...rowCards.map((card) => card.getBoundingClientRect().top),
   113	  );
   114	
   115	  window.scrollTo({
   116	    behavior: "smooth",
   117	    top: window.scrollY + rowTop - getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset(),
   118	  });
   119	
   120	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", `#${caseStudyId}`);
   121	}
   122

   128	const iosLogoSize = {
   129	  height: 10.47816,
   130	  width: 10.47816,
   131	};

   139	      width={androidLogoSize.width}
   140	      x={x}
   141	      y={y}
   142	    />
   143	  );
   144	}
   145	
   146	function PhoneAndroidIcon({ slot }: { slot: "single" | "top" }) {
   147	  const y = slot === "single" ? 11.43286 : 6.93286;
   148	
   149	  return <AndroidLogoImage x={6.23608} y={y} />;
   150	}

   152	function IosLogoImage({ x, y }: { x: number; y: number }) {

   154	    <image
   155	      height={iosLogoSize.height}

   157	      preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
   158	      width={iosLogoSize.width}
   159	      x={x}
   160	      y={y}
   161	    />
   162	  );
   163	}
   164	
   165	function PhoneAppleIcon({ slot }: { slot: "bottom" | "single" }) {
   166	  const y = slot === "single" ? 11.27372 : 17.47372;
   167	
   168	  return <IosLogoImage x={5.92372} y={y} />;
   169	}
   170	
   171	function PhoneMark({
   172	  mobilePlatforms,
   173	}: {
   174	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   175	}) {
   176	  return (
   177	    <svg
   178	      aria-hidden
   179	      className="carousel-button-phone"
   180	      height="1.936em"
   181	      style={{ height: "1.936em", width: "1.36125em" }}
   182	      viewBox="0 0 22.5 32"
   183	      width="1.36125em"
   184	    >
   185	      <rect
   186	        className="phone-mini-frame"
   187	        height="29.2"
   188	        rx="2.7"
   189	        width="19"
   190	        x="1.75"
   191	        y="1.4"
   192	      />
   193	      <rect
   194	        className="phone-mini-screen"
   195	        height="22.8"
   196	        rx="1.4"
   197	        width="14.5"
   198	        x="4"
   199	        y="4.6"
   200	      />
   201	      <path className="phone-mini-speaker" d="M8.875 3.2h4.75" />
   202	      <circle className="phone-mini-home" cx="11.25" cy="29" r="0.7" />
   203	      {mobilePlatforms.map((platform, index) => {
   204	        const slot =
   205	          mobilePlatforms.length === 1
   206	            ? "single"
   207	            : index === 0
   208	              ? "top"
   209	              : "bottom";
   210	
   211	        return platform === "android" ? (
   212	          <PhoneAndroidIcon
   213	            key={platform}
   214	            slot={slot === "bottom" ? "top" : slot}
   215	          />
   216	        ) : (
   217	          <PhoneAppleIcon
   218	            key={platform}
   219	            slot={slot === "top" ? "bottom" : slot}
   220	          />
   221	        );
   222	      })}
   223	    </svg>
   224	  );
   225	}

   275	function WebMark({ scale = 1 }: { scale?: number }) {
   276	  const size = `${(1.2 * scale).toFixed(3)}em`;
   277	
   278	  return (
   279	    <svg
   280	      aria-hidden
   281	      className="carousel-button-web"
   282	      height={size}
   283	      style={{ height: size, width: size }}
   284	      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
   285	      width={size}
   286	    >
   287	      <circle className="web-globe-shell" cx="12" cy="12" r="8.2" />
   288	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M3.8 12h16.4" />
   289	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M5.6 8.1h12.8" />
   290	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M5.6 15.9h12.8" />
   291	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M12 3.8c2.2 2.3 3.3 5 3.3 8.2s-1.1 5.9-3.3 8.2" />
   292	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M12 3.8c-2.2 2.3-3.3 5-3.3 8.2s1.1 5.9 3.3 8.2" />
   293	    </svg>
   294	  );
   295	}
   296	
   297	const platformLabelColumnStart = 3;
   298	
   299	function getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index: number) {
   300	  return platformLabelColumnStart + index * 2;
   301	}
   302	
   303	function getPlatformDividerGridColumn(index: number) {
   304	  return getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index) + 1;
   305	}
   306	
   307	function getPlatformListGridTemplate(columnCount: number) {
   308	  const columns = [
   309	    "var(--case-platform-heading-column-width, 92px)",
   310	    "minmax(8px, 1fr)",
   311	  ];
   312	
   313	  Array.from({ length: columnCount }).forEach((_, index) => {
   314	    columns.push("max-content");
   315	
   316	    if (index < columnCount - 1) {
   317	      columns.push("0px");
   318	    }
   319	  });
   320	
   321	  return columns.join(" ");
   322	}
   323	
   324	function PlatformIconSlot({
   325	  brandMark,
   326	  deviceMark,
   327	  gridColumn,
   328	  gridRow,
   329	  label,
   330	  slotIndex,
   331	}: {
   332	  brandMark?: "android" | "apple" | "web";
   333	  deviceMark?: "phone" | "tablet" | "desktop";
   334	  gridColumn?: number;
   335	  gridRow?: number;
   336	  label: string;
   337	  slotIndex?: number;
   338	}) {
   339	  return (
   340	    <span
   341	      className="case-card-title-platform-slot"
   342	      data-platform-brand-mark={brandMark}
   343	      data-platform-device-mark={deviceMark}
   344	      data-platform-index={slotIndex}
   345	      data-platform-label={brandMark || deviceMark ? "" : label}
   346	      data-platform-row={gridRow}
   347	      style={
   348	        gridColumn && gridRow
   349	          ? {
   350	              gridColumn: String(gridColumn),
   351	              gridRow: String(gridRow),
   352	            }
   353	          : undefined
   354	      }
   355	    >
   356	      {deviceMark ? (
   357	        <FactorDeviceMark device={deviceMark} />
   358	      ) : brandMark === "web" ? (
   359	        <WebMark scale={0.72} />
   360	      ) : brandMark ? (
   361	        <img
   362	          alt=""
   363	          aria-hidden="true"
   364	          className={`case-card-title-platform-brand-mark case-card-title-platform-brand-mark-${brandMark}`}
   365	          height={brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16}
   366	          src={
   367	            brandMark === "android"
   368	              ? "/images/android-robot-head.svg"
   369	              : "/images/apple-logo-official.svg"
   370	          }
   371	          style={{
   372	            height: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   373	            maxHeight: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   374	            maxWidth: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   375	            width: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   376	          }}
   377	          width={brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16}
   378	        />
   379	      ) : null}
   380	    </span>
   381	  );
   382	}
   383	
   384	function PlatformLabelDivider({
   385	  className,
   386	  gridColumn,
   387	  gridRow,
   388	  kind,
   389	}: {
   390	  className?: string;
   391	  gridColumn?: number;
   392	  gridRow?: number | string;
   393	  kind: "grid" | "item";
   394	}) {
   395	  const dividerClassName = [
   396	    `case-card-title-platform-divider case-card-title-platform-${kind}-divider`,
   397	    className,
   398	  ]
   399	    .filter(Boolean)
   400	    .join(" ");
   401	
   402	  return (
   403	    <span
   404	      aria-hidden="true"
   405	      className={dividerClassName}
   406	      style={
   407	        gridColumn && gridRow
   408	          ? {
   409	              gridColumn: String(gridColumn),
   410	              gridRow: String(gridRow),
   411	            }
   412	          : undefined
   413	      }
   414	    />
   415	  );
   416	}
   417	
   418	function PlatformLabelSlots({
   419	  useDeviceMarks,
   420	  useBrandMarks,
   421	  labels,
   422	  row,
   423	}: {
   424	  useBrandMarks?: boolean;
   425	  useDeviceMarks?: boolean;
   426	  labels: string[];
   427	  row: number;
   428	}) {
   429	  return labels.map((label, index) => (
   430	    <PlatformIconSlot
   431	      gridColumn={getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index)}
   432	      gridRow={row}
   433	      key={`${row}-${label}`}
   434	      label={label}
   435	      brandMark={
   436	        useBrandMarks
   437	          ? label === "AND"
   438	            ? "android"
   439	            : label === "IOS"
   440	              ? "apple"
   441	              : label === "WEB"
   442	                ? "web"
   443	                : undefined
   444	          : undefined
   445	      }
   446	      deviceMark={
   447	        useDeviceMarks
   448	          ? label === "PHONE"
   449	              ? "phone"
   450	            : label === "TABLET"
   451	              ? "tablet"
   452	              : label === "DESK"
   453	                ? "desktop"
   454	                : undefined
   455	          : undefined
   456	      }
   457	      slotIndex={index}
   458	    />
   459	  ));
   460	}
   461	
   462	function PlatformColumnDividers({
   463	  columnCount,
   464	  gridRow,
   465	  partial,
   466	  startIndex = 0,
   467	}: {
   468	  columnCount: number;
   469	  gridRow: number | string;
   470	  partial?: boolean;
   471	  startIndex?: number;
   472	}) {
   473	  return Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, columnCount - 1 - startIndex) }).map(
   474	    (_, index) => {
   475	      const dividerIndex = startIndex + index;
   476	
   477	      return (
   478	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   479	          className={
   480	            partial
   481	              ? "case-card-title-platform-partial-divider"
   482	              : undefined
   483	          }
   484	          gridColumn={getPlatformDividerGridColumn(dividerIndex)}
   485	          gridRow={gridRow}
   486	          key={`divider-${gridRow}-${dividerIndex}`}
   487	          kind="item"
   488	        />
   489	      );
   490	    },
   491	  );
   492	}

   556	function PlatformLogoMark({ platform }: { platform: MobilePlatform }) {
   557	  return platform === "android" ? (
   558	    <AndroidPlatformMark />
   559	  ) : (
   560	    <ApplePlatformMark />
   561	  );

   563	
   564	function FactorDeviceMark({
   565	  device,
   566	}: {
   567	  device: "phone" | "tablet" | "desktop";
   568	}) {
   569	  if (device === "phone") {
   570	    return <PhoneMark mobilePlatforms={[]} />;
   571	  }
   572	
   573	  if (device === "tablet") {
   574	    return <TabletMark showPlatformIcon={false} />;
   575	  }
   576	
   577	  return (
   578	    <img
   579	      alt=""
   580	      aria-hidden="true"
   581	      className="case-card-title-desktop-mark"
   582	      src="/images/freeicons-chip-component-white.svg"
   583	    />
   584	  );
   585	}
   586	
   587	export function ProjectDeviceStack({
   588	  assetGap,
   589	  color,
   590	  hasTablet,
   591	  hasWeb,
   592	  deviceLabels,
   593	  mobilePlatforms,
   594	  platformLabels: customPlatformLabels,
   595	  usePlatformBrandMarks,
   596	  responsiveAssetGap,
   597	  showPlatformLabels,
   598	  webIconScale,
   599	}: {
   600	  assetGap?: string;
   601	  color: string;
   602	  deviceLabels?: string[];
   603	  hasTablet?: boolean;
   604	  hasWeb?: boolean;
   605	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   606	  platformLabels?: string[];
   607	  usePlatformBrandMarks?: boolean;
   608	  responsiveAssetGap?: ResponsiveAssetGap;
   609	  showPlatformLabels?: boolean;
   610	  webIconScale?: number;
   611	}) {
   612	  const stackRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
   613	  const [measuredAssetGap, setMeasuredAssetGap] = useState<string | null>(null);
   614	  const mobilePlatformKey = mobilePlatforms.join(",");
   615	  const stackStyle: CSSProperties & {
   616	    "--project-device-stack-gap"?: string;
   617	  } = {
   618	    color,
   619	  };
   620	  const stackGap = measuredAssetGap ?? assetGap;
   621	  const deviceLabelKey = deviceLabels?.join(",") ?? "";
   622	  const platformLabelKey = customPlatformLabels?.join(",") ?? "";
   623	
   624	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   625	    if (!responsiveAssetGap) {
   626	      setMeasuredAssetGap(null);
   627	      return;
   628	    }
   629	
   630	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   631	    const measurementTarget = stack?.parentElement ?? stack;
   632	
   633	    if (!measurementTarget) {
   634	      return;
   635	    }
   636	
   637	    let frame = 0;
   638	    const updateGap = () => {
   639	      if (frame) {
   640	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   641	      }
   642	
   643	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   644	        const laneWidth = measurementTarget.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   645	        const activeRange = Math.max(
   646	          1,
   647	          responsiveAssetGap.wideLanePx - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx,
   648	        );
   649	        const progress = Math.min(
   650	          1,
   651	          Math.max(
   652	            0,
   653	            (laneWidth - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx) / activeRange,
   654	          ),
   655	        );
   656	        const nextGap =
   657	          responsiveAssetGap.minPx +
   658	          (responsiveAssetGap.maxPx - responsiveAssetGap.minPx) * progress;
   659	        const nextGapValue = `${nextGap.toFixed(2)}px`;
   660	
   661	        setMeasuredAssetGap((currentGap) =>
   662	          currentGap === nextGapValue ? currentGap : nextGapValue,
   663	        );
   664	      });
   665	    };
   666	
   667	    updateGap();
   668	
   669	    const resizeObserver =
   670	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   671	        ? null
   672	        : new ResizeObserver(updateGap);
   673	
   674	    resizeObserver?.observe(measurementTarget);
   675	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   676	
   677	    return () => {
   678	      if (frame) {
   679	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   680	      }
   681	
   682	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   683	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   684	    };
   685	  }, [
   686	    responsiveAssetGap?.maxPx,
   687	    responsiveAssetGap?.minPx,
   688	    responsiveAssetGap?.tightLanePx,
   689	    responsiveAssetGap?.wideLanePx,
   690	  ]);
   691	
   692	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   693	    if (!showPlatformLabels) {
   694	      return;
   695	    }
   696	
   697	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   698	    const rowDivider = stack?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   699	      ".case-card-title-platform-row-divider",
   700	    );
   701	
   702	    if (!stack || !rowDivider) {
   703	      return;
   704	    }
   705	
   706	    let frame = 0;
   707	    const updateRowDivider = () => {
   708	      if (frame) {
   709	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   710	      }
   711	
   712	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   713	        const platformHeading = stack.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   714	          ".case-card-title-platform-heading-platform",
   715	        );
   716	        const deviceHeading = stack.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   717	          ".case-card-title-platform-heading-factor",
   718	        );
   719	        const verticalDividers = Array.from(
   720	          stack.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
   721	            ".case-card-title-platform-divider",
   722	          ),
   723	        );
   724	
   725	        if (
   726	          !platformHeading ||
   727	          !deviceHeading ||
   728	          verticalDividers.length === 0
   729	        ) {
   730	          stack.removeAttribute("data-platform-row-divider-ready");
   731	          return;
   732	        }
   733	
   734	        const stackRect = stack.getBoundingClientRect();
   735	        const platformRect = platformHeading.getBoundingClientRect();
   736	        const deviceRect = deviceHeading.getBoundingClientRect();
   737	        const dividerRects = verticalDividers.map((divider) =>
   738	          divider.getBoundingClientRect(),
   739	        );
   740	        const leftmostDividerCenter = Math.min(
   741	          ...dividerRects.map((rect) => rect.left + rect.width / 2),
   742	        );
   743	        const rightmostDividerCenter = Math.max(
   744	          ...dividerRects.map((rect) => rect.left + rect.width / 2),
   745	        );
   746	        const dividerStartCenter = deviceRect.left + deviceRect.width / 2;
   747	        const dividerOverhang = Math.max(
   748	          0,
   749	          leftmostDividerCenter - dividerStartCenter,
   750	        );
   751	        const dividerEndCenter = rightmostDividerCenter + dividerOverhang;
   752	        const dividerStart = dividerStartCenter - stackRect.left;
   753	        const dividerEnd = dividerEndCenter - stackRect.left;
   754	        const dividerTop =
   755	          (platformRect.top +
   756	            platformRect.height / 2 +
   757	            deviceRect.top +
   758	            deviceRect.height / 2) /
   759	            2 -
   760	          stackRect.top;
   761	        const dividerWidth = Math.max(0, dividerEnd - dividerStart);
   762	
   763	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   764	          "--case-platform-row-divider-left",
   765	          `${dividerStart.toFixed(2)}px`,
   766	        );
   767	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   768	          "--case-platform-row-divider-top",
   769	          `${dividerTop.toFixed(2)}px`,
   770	        );
   771	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   772	          "--case-platform-row-divider-width",
   773	          `${dividerWidth.toFixed(2)}px`,
   774	        );
   775	        stack.toggleAttribute(
   776	          "data-platform-row-divider-ready",
   777	          dividerWidth > 0,
   778	        );
   779	      });
   780	    };
   781	
   782	    updateRowDivider();
   783	    document.fonts?.ready.then(updateRowDivider);
   784	
   785	    const resizeObserver =
   786	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   787	        ? null
   788	        : new ResizeObserver(updateRowDivider);
   789	
   790	    resizeObserver?.observe(stack);
   791	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateRowDivider);
   792	
   793	    return () => {
   794	      if (frame) {
   795	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   796	      }
   797	
   798	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   799	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateRowDivider);
   800	    };
   801	  }, [
   802	    deviceLabelKey,
   803	    hasTablet,
   804	    hasWeb,
   805	    mobilePlatformKey,
   806	    platformLabelKey,
   807	    showPlatformLabels,
   808	    stackGap,
   809	  ]);
   810	
   811	  if (stackGap) {
   812	    stackStyle["--project-device-stack-gap"] = stackGap;
   813	  }
   814	
   815	  if (showPlatformLabels) {
   816	    const platforms: Array<MobilePlatform | "web"> = [];
   817	    const factorDevices: Array<"phone" | "tablet"> = [];
   818	
   819	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("android") || hasTablet) {
   820	      platforms.push("android");
   821	    }
   822	
   823	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("apple")) {
   824	      platforms.push("apple");
   825	    }
   826	
   827	    if (hasWeb) {
   828	      platforms.push("web");
   829	    }
   830	
   831	    if (mobilePlatforms.length > 0) {
   832	      factorDevices.push("phone");
   833	    }
   834	
   835	    if (hasTablet) {
   836	      factorDevices.push("tablet");
   837	    }
   838	
   839	    const platformLabels =
   840	      customPlatformLabels ??
   841	      platforms.map((platform) =>
   842	        platform === "web" ? "WEB" : platform === "android" ? "AND" : "IOS",
   843	      );
   844	    const factorDeviceLabels =
   845	      deviceLabels ??
   846	      factorDevices.map((device) =>
   847	        device === "phone" ? "PHONE" : "TABLET",
   848	      );
   849	    const labelColumnCount = Math.max(
   850	      platformLabels.length,
   851	      factorDeviceLabels.length,
   852	      1,
   853	    );
   854	    const dividerGridRow = factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? "1 / 3" : 1;
   855	    const spanningItemDividerCount =
   856	      factorDeviceLabels.length > platformLabels.length
   857	        ? platformLabels.length
   858	        : Math.max(0, platformLabels.length - 1);
   859	    const platformListStyle = {
   860	      ...stackStyle,
   861	      gridTemplateColumns: getPlatformListGridTemplate(labelColumnCount),
   862	    };
   863	
   864	    return (
   865	      <span
   866	        className="carousel-button-project case-card-title-platform-list"
   867	        ref={stackRef}
   868	        style={platformListStyle}
   869	      >
   870	        <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-platform">
   871	          PLATFORM
   872	        </span>
   873	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   874	          gridColumn={2}
   875	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   876	          kind="grid"
   877	        />
   878	        <PlatformLabelSlots
   879	          labels={platformLabels}
   880	          row={1}
   881	          useBrandMarks={usePlatformBrandMarks}
   882	        />
   883	        <PlatformColumnDividers
   884	          columnCount={spanningItemDividerCount + 1}
   885	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   886	        />
   887	        {factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? (
   888	          <>
   889	            <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-factor">
   890	              DEVICE
   891	            </span>
   892	            <PlatformLabelSlots
   893	              labels={factorDeviceLabels}
   894	              row={2}
   895	              useDeviceMarks
   896	            />
   897	            <PlatformColumnDividers
   898	              columnCount={factorDeviceLabels.length}
   899	              gridRow={2}
   900	              partial
   901	              startIndex={spanningItemDividerCount}
   902	            />
   903	            <span
   904	              aria-hidden="true"
   905	              className="case-card-title-platform-row-divider"
   906	            />
   907	          </>
   908	        ) : null}
   909	      </span>
   910	    );
   911	  }
   912	
   913	  return (
   914	    <span className="carousel-button-project" ref={stackRef} style={stackStyle}>
   915	      {mobilePlatforms.map((platform) => (
   916	        <PhoneMark key={platform} mobilePlatforms={[platform]} />
   917	      ))}
   918	      {hasTablet ? <TabletMark /> : null}
   919	      {hasWeb ? <WebMark scale={webIconScale} /> : null}
   920	    </span>
   921	  );
   922	}
   923	
   924	function CarouselArrowMark() {
   925	  return (
   926	    <svg
   927	      aria-hidden
   928	      className="carousel-button-arrow-mark"
   929	      viewBox="0 0 32 24"
   930	      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
   931	    >
   932	      <path
   933	        d="M8.4 12H26M18 4l8 8-8 8"
   934	        fill="none"
   935	        stroke="#000"
   936	        strokeLinecap="round"
   937	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   938	        strokeWidth="6.2"
   939	      />
   940	      <path
   941	        d="M8.4 12H26M18 4l8 8-8 8"
   942	        fill="none"
   943	        stroke="#f3fff7"
   944	        strokeLinecap="round"
   945	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   946	        strokeWidth="3.6"
   947	      />
   948	    </svg>
   949	  );
   950	}
   951	
   952	export function ProjectCarouselButton() {
   953	  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
   954	  const projectButtonRef = useRef<HTMLAnchorElement>(null);
   955	  const activeProject = projectLinks[activeIndex];
   956	
   957	  const handleArrowClick = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
   958	    event.preventDefault();
   959	    scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(activeProject.caseStudyId);
   960	  };
   961	
   962	  useEffect(() => {
   963	    const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
   964	      setActiveIndex((currentIndex) => (currentIndex + 1) % projectLinks.length);
   965	    }, 5000);
   966	
   967	    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
   968	  }, []);
   969	
   970	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   971	    let frame = 0;
   972	
   973	    const syncHeroActionGeometry = () => {
   974	      if (frame) {
   975	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   976	      }
   977	
   978	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   979	        const heroCopy = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-copy");
   980	        const heroActions =
   981	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
   982	        const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
   983	        const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
   984	        const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
   985	        const phoneStage = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".phone-stage");
   986	        const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
   987	        const profilePhoto =
   988	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
   989	        const socialButton =
   990	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-social-button");
   991	        const visualFrameSelectors = [
   992	          ".profile-photo",
   993	          ".phone-shell-commerce",
   994	          ".phone-shell-finance",
   995	          ".tablet-shell",
   996	        ];
   997	
   998	        if (proofPanel && profilePhoto) {
   999	          const proofRect = proofPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
  1000	          const profileRect = profilePhoto.getBoundingClientRect();
  1001	          const proofWidth = profileRect.right - proofRect.left;
  1002	
  1003	          if (proofWidth > 0) {
  1004	            [heroCopy, proofPanel, stackRow].forEach((element) => {
  1005	              element?.style.setProperty(
  1006	                "--hero-proof-landscape-width",
  1007	                `${proofWidth}px`,
  1008	              );
  1009	            });
  1010	          }
  1011	        }
  1012	
  1013	        if (heroVisual && navBar && stackRow) {
  1014	          const isTwoColumnHero = window.matchMedia(
  1015	            "(min-width: 1041px), (min-width: 981px) and (orientation: landscape)",
  1016	          ).matches;
  1017	
  1018	          if (!isTwoColumnHero) {
  1019	            heroVisual.style.removeProperty("--hero-visual-center-offset");
  1020	          } else if (window.scrollY <= 1) {
  1021	            const isCompactLandscapeHero = window.matchMedia(
  1022	              "(min-width: 981px) and (max-width: 1023px) and (orientation: landscape)",
  1023	            ).matches;
  1024	            const heroVisualScale =
  1025	              heroVisual.offsetWidth > 0
  1026	                ? heroVisual.getBoundingClientRect().width / heroVisual.offsetWidth
  1027	                : 1;
  1028	            const deviceStageSnap = isCompactLandscapeHero
  1029	              ? (Number.parseFloat(
  1030	                  window
  1031	                    .getComputedStyle(phoneStage ?? heroVisual)
  1032	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-device-stage-snap"),
  1033	                ) || 0) * heroVisualScale
  1034	              : 0;
  1035	            const visualRects = visualFrameSelectors
  1036	              .map((selector) => ({
  1037	                element: document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector),
  1038	                selector,
  1039	              }))
  1040	              .filter(
  1041	                (
  1042	                  entry,
  1043	                ): entry is { element: HTMLElement; selector: string } =>
  1044	                  Boolean(entry.element),
  1045	              )
  1046	              .map(({ element, selector }) => {
  1047	                const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  1048	                const snapAdjustment =
  1049	                  selector === ".profile-photo" ? 0 : deviceStageSnap;
  1050	
  1051	                return {
  1052	                  bottom: rect.bottom - snapAdjustment,
  1053	                  top: rect.top - snapAdjustment,
  1054	                };
  1055	              });
  1056	
  1057	            if (visualRects.length > 0) {
  1058	              const navRect = navBar.getBoundingClientRect();
  1059	              const stackRect = stackRow.getBoundingClientRect();
  1060	              const visualTop = Math.min(
  1061	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.top),
  1062	              );
  1063	              const visualBottom = Math.max(
  1064	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.bottom),
  1065	              );
  1066	              const currentOffset =
  1067	                Number.parseFloat(
  1068	                  window
  1069	                    .getComputedStyle(heroVisual)
  1070	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-visual-center-offset"),
  1071	                ) || 0;
  1072	              const visualCenterWithoutOffset =
  1073	                (visualTop + visualBottom) / 2 - currentOffset;
  1074	              const targetCenter = (navRect.bottom + stackRect.top) / 2;
  1075	              const nextOffset = targetCenter - visualCenterWithoutOffset;
  1076	
  1077	              heroVisual.style.setProperty(
  1078	                "--hero-visual-center-offset",
  1079	                `${nextOffset}px`,
  1080	              );
  1081	            }
  1082	          }
  1083	        }
  1084	
  1085	        if (heroActions && socialButton) {
  1086	          const heroActionsWidth = heroActions.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  1087	          const socialButtonHeight =
  1088	            socialButton.getBoundingClientRect().height;
  1089	
  1090	          if (heroCopy && heroActionsWidth > 0) {
  1091	            heroCopy.style.setProperty(
  1092	              "--hero-text-stack-width",
  1093	              `${heroActionsWidth}px`,
  1094	            );
  1095	          }
  1096	
  1097	          if (socialButtonHeight > 0) {
  1098	            const setPx = (name: string, value: number) => {
  1099	              heroActions.style.setProperty(name, `${value}px`);
  1100	            };
  1101	
  1102	            setPx("--hero-social-button-height", socialButtonHeight);
  1103	            setPx("--hero-social-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.295833);
  1104	            setPx("--hero-social-icon-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.672);
  1105	            const baseSocialButtonHeight = 48;
  1106	            const baseSocialGap = baseSocialButtonHeight * 0.045833 + 1;
  1107	            const stretchedSocialGap =
  1108	              Math.max(0, socialButtonHeight - baseSocialButtonHeight) *
  1109	              0.091666;
  1110	
  1111	            setPx("--hero-social-gap", baseSocialGap + stretchedSocialGap);
  1112	            setPx("--hero-social-pad-x", socialButtonHeight / 8);
  1113	            setPx("--hero-view-label-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.275);
  1114	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
  1115	              "--hero-eyebrow-font-size",
  1116	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.367}px`,
  1117	            );
  1118	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
  1119	              "--hero-lede-font-size",
  1120	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.5}px`,
  1121	            );
  1122	            setPx(
  1123	              "--hero-carousel-base-font-size",
  1124	              socialButtonHeight * 0.34375,
  1125	            );
  1126	            setPx(
  1127	              "--hero-carousel-name-font-size",
  1128	              socialButtonHeight * 0.352917,
  1129	            );
  1130	            setPx(
  1131	              "--hero-carousel-category-font-size",
  1132	              socialButtonHeight * 0.176458,
  1133	            );
  1134	            setPx(
  1135	              "--hero-carousel-arrow-font-size",
  1136	              socialButtonHeight * 0.458333,
  1137	            );
  1138	          }
  1139	        }
  1140	
  1141	        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("hero-stack-sync"));
  1142	
  1143	        const viewLabel =
  1144	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
  1145	        const githubText =
  1146	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
  1147	        const githubIcon =
  1148	          document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
  1149	
  1150	        if (viewLabel && githubText && githubIcon) {
  1151	          const viewLabelRect = viewLabel.getBoundingClientRect();
  1152	          const githubTextRect = githubText.getBoundingClientRect();
  1153	          const githubIconRect = githubIcon.getBoundingClientRect();
  1154	          const clusterLeft = Math.min(githubTextRect.left, githubIconRect.left);
  1155	          const stackLeft = clusterLeft - viewLabelRect.left;
  1156	
  1157	          viewLabel.style.setProperty(
  1158	            "--hero-view-stack-left",
  1159	            `${stackLeft}px`,
  1160	          );
  1161	        }
  1162	      });
  1163	    };
  1164	
  1165	    syncHeroActionGeometry();
  1166	    window.addEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1167	
  1168	    const targetIcon =
  1169	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".dribbble-icon-mark") ??
  1170	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".linkedin-icon-mark");
  1171	    const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
  1172	    const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
  1173	    const heroActions = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
  1174	    const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
  1175	    const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
  1176	    const profilePhoto = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
  1177	    const targetButton = targetIcon?.closest(".hero-social-button");
  1178	    const viewLabel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
  1179	    const githubText = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
  1180	    const githubIcon =
  1181	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
  1182	    const githubButton = githubText?.closest(".github-button");
  1183	    const resizeObserver =
  1184	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
  1185	        ? null
  1186	        : new ResizeObserver(syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1187	
  1188	    if (resizeObserver) {
  1189	      if (projectButtonRef.current) {
  1190	        resizeObserver.observe(projectButtonRef.current);
  1191	      }
  1192	
  1193	      if (heroActions) {
  1194	        resizeObserver.observe(heroActions);
  1195	      }
  1196	
  1197	      if (heroVisual) {
  1198	        resizeObserver.observe(heroVisual);
  1199	      }
  1200	
  1201	      if (stackRow) {
  1202	        resizeObserver.observe(stackRow);
  1203	      }
  1204	
  1205	      if (proofPanel) {
  1206	        resizeObserver.observe(proofPanel);
  1207	      }
  1208	
  1209	      if (profilePhoto) {
  1210	        resizeObserver.observe(profilePhoto);
  1211	      }
  1212	
  1213	      if (targetIcon) {
  1214	        resizeObserver.observe(targetIcon);
  1215	      }
  1216	
  1217	      if (targetButton) {
  1218	        resizeObserver.observe(targetButton);
  1219	      }
  1220	
  1221	      if (viewLabel) {
  1222	        resizeObserver.observe(viewLabel);
  1223	      }
  1224	
  1225	      if (githubText) {
  1226	        resizeObserver.observe(githubText);
  1227	      }
  1228	
  1229	      if (githubIcon) {
  1230	        resizeObserver.observe(githubIcon);
  1231	      }
  1232	
  1233	      if (githubButton) {
  1234	        resizeObserver.observe(githubButton);
  1235	      }
  1236	    }
  1237	
  1238	    if ("fonts" in document) {
  1239	      document.fonts.ready.then(syncHeroActionGeometry).catch(() => {});
  1240	    }
  1241	
  1242	    return () => {
  1243	      if (frame) {
  1244	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  1245	      }
  1246	
  1247	      window.removeEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1248	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
  1249	    };
  1250	  }, []);
  1251	
  1252	  return (
  1253	    <span className="carousel-button-group">
  1254	      <a
  1255	        aria-label={`Open ${activeProject.name} project`}
  1256	        className="button button-primary carousel-button"
  1257	        href={activeProject.href}
  1258	        ref={projectButtonRef}
  1259	        rel="noreferrer"
  1260	        target="_blank"
  1261	      >
  1262	        <span className="carousel-button-window" aria-live="polite">
  1263	          <span className="carousel-button-label" key={activeProject.href}>
  1264	            <span
  1265	              className={`carousel-button-action carousel-button-action-${activeProject.tone}`}
  1266	              style={{
  1267	                ...strokedWhiteTextStyle,
  1268	                alignItems: "center",
  1269	                color: projectNameColors[activeProject.tone],
  1270	                display: "inline-flex",
  1271	                flexDirection: "column",
  1272	                fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-name-font-size)",
  1273	                gap: "2.2px",
  1274	                justifyContent: "center",
  1275	                lineHeight: 0.95,
  1276	              }}
  1277	            >
  1278	              <span
  1279	                className="carousel-button-action-name"
  1280	                style={{ fontSize: activeProject.nameFontSize }}
  1281	              >
  1282	                {activeProject.name}
  1283	              </span>
  1284	              <span
  1285	                className="carousel-button-action-category"
  1286	                style={{
  1287	                  WebkitTextStroke: 0,
  1288	                  color: "#000",
  1289	                  fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-category-font-size)",
  1290	                  fontWeight: 900,
  1291	                  letterSpacing: "0.06em",
  1292	                  lineHeight: 1,
  1293	                  textAlign: "center",
  1294	                  textShadow: "none",
  1295	                }}
  1296	              >
  1297	                {activeProject.category}
  1298	              </span>
  1299	            </span>
  1300	            <ProjectDeviceStack
  1301	              color={projectNameColors[activeProject.tone]}
  1302	              hasTablet={activeProject.hasTablet}
  1303	              hasWeb={activeProject.hasWeb}
  1304	              mobilePlatforms={activeProject.mobilePlatforms}
  1305	            />
  1306	          </span>
  1307	        </span>
  1308	      </a>
  1309	      <a
  1310	        aria-label={`Scroll to ${activeProject.name} case study`}
  1311	        className="button button-primary carousel-arrow-button"
  1312	        href={`#${activeProject.caseStudyId}`}
  1313	        onClick={handleArrowClick}
  1314	      >
  1315	        <span aria-hidden className="carousel-button-arrow">
  1316	          <CarouselArrowMark />
  1317	        </span>
  1318	      </a>
  1319	    </span>
  1320	  );
  1321	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Prahl.dev Portfolio Site
     2	
     3	Next.js + Tailwind CSS portfolio start page for Prahl.dev.
     4	
     5	## What is included
     6	
     7	- `app/page.tsx` - the homepage/start page.
     8	- `app/globals.css` - the green/black visual system and responsive layout.
     9	- `public/images/profile-placeholder.png` - generated AI profile placeholder; replace this with your own square photo later using the same filename.
    10	- `public/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png` - the original generated mockup reference.
    11	- `public/favicon.svg` - simple green/black monogram favicon.
    12	
    13	## Local development
    14	
    15	```sh
    16	npm install
    17	npm run dev
    18	```
    19	
    20	Open `http://localhost:3000`.
    21	
    22	## Production build
    23	
    24	For Vercel, Netlify, or another Next.js host:
    25	
    26	```sh
    27	npm run build
    28	```
    29	
    30	For a simple static host:
    31	
    32	```sh
    33	npm run build:static
    34	```
    35	
    36	Then upload the generated `out/` folder.
    37	
    38	## Replace the profile photo
    39	
    40	Put your real square profile photo at:
    41	
    42	```txt
    43	public/images/profile-placeholder.png
    44	```
    45	
    46	Keep the filename the same and rebuild.


============================================================
FILE: DEPLOYMENT.md
============================================================

     1	# Deployment Notes
     2	
     3	## Best Option: Vercel
     4	
     5	1. Upload this `portfolio-site` folder to a GitHub repo.
     6	2. Import the repo in Vercel.
     7	3. Use the default Next.js settings.
     8	4. Build command: `npm run build`.
     9	
    10	## Netlify
    11	
    12	1. Upload this folder to a GitHub repo.
    13	2. Connect the repo in Netlify.
    14	3. Build command: `npm run build`.
    15	4. Publish directory: `.next`.
    16	5. Enable Netlify's Next.js runtime if prompted.
    17	
    18	## Simple Static Hosting
    19	
    20	Use this when the host only accepts HTML/CSS/JS files:
    21	
    22	```sh
    23	npm install
    24	npm run build:static
    25	```
    26	
    27	Upload the contents of:
    28	
    29	```txt
    30	out/
    31	```
    32	
    33	This mode does not need a running Node.js server after upload.
    34	
    35	## Contact Button
    36	
    37	The contact button currently uses:
    38	
    39	```txt
    40	mailto:hello@example.com
    41	```
    42	
    43	Replace `hello@example.com` in `app/page.tsx` with your real email address.

FILE: .github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml
============================================================

     1	name: Build static export for Hostinger
     2	
     3	on:
     4	  push:
     5	    branches: [main]
     6	  workflow_dispatch:
     7	
     8	permissions:
     9	  contents: write
    10	
    11	concurrency:
    12	  group: hostinger-static-export
    13	  cancel-in-progress: true
    14	
    15	jobs:
    16	  build-and-publish:
    17	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    18	    steps:
    19	      - name: Check out source
    20	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    21	
    22	      - name: Set up Node
    23	        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
    24	        with:
    25	          node-version: 22
    26	          cache: npm
    27	
    28	      - name: Install dependencies
    29	        run: npm ci
    30	
    31	      - name: Build static export
    32	        run: npm run build:static
    33	
    34	      - name: Publish static export branch
    35	        env:
    36	          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    37	        run: |
    38	          cd out
    39	          git init -b hostinger-deploy
    40	          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
    41	          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
    42	          git add -A
    43	          git commit -m "Deploy static export from ${GITHUB_SHA}"
    44	          git remote add origin "https://x-access-token:${GITHUB_TOKEN}@github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}.git"
    45	          git push --force origin hostinger-deploy

============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/portfolio_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Portfolio project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Portfolio Next.js PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you do not understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	10. For this Portfolio repo/website project, always ask me for the commit message before committing or pushing.
    28	
    29	
    30	============================================================
    31	PORTFOLIO PROJECT NOTES
    32	============================================================
    33	
    34	- Main source branch: main
    35	- Deployment source: GitHub Actions builds main into the hostinger-deploy branch
    36	- Hostinger root directory: public_html
    37	- Main stack represented by the website: React / React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, Kotlin
    38	- Public website assets live under public/
    39	- Run this script before starting a Portfolio session to refresh the project snapshot.
    40	
    41	
    42	============================================================
    43	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    44	============================================================
    45	
    46	This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    47	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    48	
    49	The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
    50	- .git
    51	- node_modules
    52	- .next
    53	- .next.*
    54	- out
    55	- dist
    56	- build
    57	- .cache
    58	- .turbo
    59	- .npm
    60	- .pnpm-store
    61	- .yarn
    62	- .DS_Store
    63	- next-env.d.ts
    64	- *.tsbuildinfo
    65	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    66	- *.zip
    67	- .env and .env*.local
    68	
    69	Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    70	
    71	
    72	============================================================
    73	PROJECT FILE TREE
    74	============================================================
    75	
    76	EOF
    77	}
    78	
    79	print_file() {
    80	  local file="$1"
    81	
    82	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    83	    {
    84	      echo ""
    85	      echo ""
    86	      echo "============================================================"
    87	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    88	      echo "============================================================"
    89	      echo ""
    90	      nl -ba "$file"
    91	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	  fi
    93	}
    94	
    95	append_project_tree() {
    96	  # Use Git's own ignore rules so the snapshot cannot drift from .gitignore.
    97	  # Include tracked files plus untracked files that Git does not ignore.
    98	  printf '%s\n' "$PROJECT_DIR" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    99	
   100	  git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard \
   101	    | sort \
   102	    | while IFS= read -r relative_file; do
   103	      case "$relative_file" in
   104	        .git/*|node_modules/*|.next/*|.next.*/*|out/*|dist/*|build/*|.cache/*|.turbo/*|.npm/*|.pnpm-store/*|.yarn/*|.DS_Store|*/.DS_Store|*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt|*.zip|.env|.env*.local|next-env.d.ts|*.tsbuildinfo)
   105	          continue
   106	          ;;
   107	      esac
   108	
   109	      printf '%s/%s\n' "$PROJECT_DIR" "$relative_file" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   110	    done
   111	}
   112	
   113	print_inventory_header() {
   114	  local title="$1"
   115	
   116	  {
   117	    echo ""
   118	    echo ""
   119	    echo "============================================================"
   120	    echo "$title"
   121	    echo "============================================================"
   122	    echo ""
   123	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   124	}
   125	
   126	print_file_inventory_line() {
   127	  local file="$1"
   128	  local size
   129	  local modified
   130	
   131	  size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   132	  modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   133	  printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   134	}
   135	
   136	print_dir_inventory() {
   137	  local title="$1"
   138	  local dir="$2"
   139	
   140	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   141	
   142	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
   143	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   144	    return
   145	  fi
   146	
   147	  find "$dir" -type f \
   148	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   149	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   150	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   151	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   152	  done
   153	}
   154	
   155	print_matching_inventory() {
   156	  local title="$1"
   157	  shift
   158	
   159	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   160	
   161	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" "$@" -type f \
   162	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git/*" \
   163	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules/*" \
   164	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next/*" \
   165	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next.*/*" \
   166	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out/*" \
   167	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   168	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   169	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   170	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   171	  done
   172	}
   173	
   174	append_git_context() {
   175	  {
   176	    echo ""
   177	    echo ""
   178	    echo "============================================================"
   179	    echo "GIT CONTEXT"
   180	    echo "============================================================"
   181	    echo ""
   182	    echo "Current branch:"
   183	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" branch --show-current 2>/dev/null || true
   184	    echo ""
   185	    echo "Status:"
   186	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" status --short --branch 2>/dev/null || true
   187	    echo ""
   188	    echo "Recent commits:"
   189	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" log --oneline --decorate -10 2>/dev/null || true
   190	    echo ""
   191	    echo "Remotes:"
   192	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" remote -v 2>/dev/null || true
   193	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   194	}
   195	
   196	write_snapshot() {
   197	  write_header
   198	
   199	  append_project_tree
   200	
   201	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   202	
   203	
   204	============================================================
   205	FILE CONTENTS - APP SOURCE
   206	============================================================
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	
   210	  for file in \
   211	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/layout.tsx" \
   212	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/page.tsx" \
   213	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/ProjectCarouselButton.tsx" \
   214	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/globals.css"
   215	  do
   216	    print_file "$file"
   217	  done
   218	
   219	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   220	
   221	
   222	============================================================
   223	FILE CONTENTS - ROOT / TOOLING / DEPLOYMENT
   224	============================================================
   225	
   226	EOF
   227	
   228	  for file in \
   229	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   230	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   231	    "$PROJECT_DIR/DEPLOYMENT.md" \
   232	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package.json" \
   233	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package-lock.json" \
   234	    "$PROJECT_DIR/next.config.ts" \
   235	    "$PROJECT_DIR/postcss.config.mjs" \
   236	    "$PROJECT_DIR/tsconfig.json" \
   237	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml" \
   238	    "$PROJECT_DIR/public/favicon.svg" \
   239	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   240	  do
   241	    print_file "$file"
   242	  done
   243	
   244	  append_git_context
   245	
   246	  print_dir_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY - PUBLIC IMAGES" "$PROJECT_DIR/public/images"
   247	  print_matching_inventory "PDF WORKLOG INVENTORY" \( -name "portfolio_worklog_*.pdf" \)
   248	  print_matching_inventory "DEPLOYMENT ARCHIVE INVENTORY" \( -name "*.zip" \)
   249	
   250	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   251	
   252	
   253	============================================================
   254	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   255	============================================================
   256	
   257	The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
   258	- .git
   259	- node_modules
   260	- .next
   261	- .next.*
   262	- out
   263	- dist
   264	- build
   265	- .cache
   266	- .turbo
   267	- .npm
   268	- .pnpm-store
   269	- .yarn
   270	- .DS_Store
   271	- next-env.d.ts
   272	- *.tsbuildinfo
   273	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   274	- *.zip
   275	- .env and .env*.local
   276	
   277	The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
   278	
   279	EOF
   280	}
   281	
   282	write_snapshot
   283	
   284	echo "Done."
   285	echo ""
   286	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   287	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   288	echo ""
   289	echo "Open it with:"
   290	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""


============================================================
GIT CONTEXT
============================================================

Current branch:
main

Status:
## main...origin/main
 M app/globals.css

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- .next.*
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
